का . पा . 2779.-- - प्रायकर अधिनियम , 1961 ( 1961 
द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार का 43) की धारा 10 के खंड ( 23 ग ) के उपखंड ( 6 ) 
एतद्वारा " श्री बाला मुरगन देवस्थानम् ट्रस्ट , अहमदाबाद द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार 
को कर निर्धारण वर्ष 1990- 91 और 1991-92 तक के लिए एतदद्वारा " श्री राघवेन्द्र स्वामी मठ, प्रांत प्रवेश को कर 
निम्नलिखित शतों के मध्यधीन रहते हए उन्त उपखंड के मिर्धारण वर्ष 1990- 91 मे 1992- 93 तक के लिए निम्न - 
योजनार्थ अधिसूचित करती है, अर्थात् : 

लिखित शर्तों के मध्यधीन रहते हुए उक्त . उपखंड के प्रयोज .. 
( 1 ) कर निर्धारिती इसकी प्राय का इस्तमाल अथवा 

नार्थ अधिसूचित करती है , अर्थात् : - - 
इसमी प्राय का इस्तेमाल करने के लिए इसका 

( 1 ) कर निर्धारिती इसकी प्राय का इस्तेमाल अथवा 
संचयन पूर्णतया तथा अनन्यतया उन उद्देश्यों के 

इसकी प्राय का इस्तेमाल करने के लिए इसका 
लिए करेगा जिनके लिए इसकी स्थापना की गई 

संचवम पूर्ण नया तथा अनन्यतया उन उद्देश्यों के लिए 

करेगा , जिनके लिए इसकी स्थापना की गई है । 
( 2 ) करनिर्धारिती ऊपर उल्लिखित करनिर्धारण वर्षों

against him . Aralgaria Project has got no certified standing

cable to that Proiect. The concerned workman replied to 
th: c charpeslicet denying each and every allegation but tho 
managemenı being not satisfied with the reply , ordered for 
derartmental enquiry which anart from perfunctory in naturo 
was irrecular and invalid . He raised industrial dispute but 
in the meantime the manacement dismissed him on 24 - 12 -86 . 
The dismissal according to the concerned workman was 
illeral, uniustificd and against the principles of natural justice . 


5 . The managoment on the other hand stated that the 
incident took placo when Shri K . P . Singh was carrying on 
his duty with the help and assistance of Shri B . S . Ram , 
Safety Officer , Shri B . N . Jha, Dy. Surveyor and Shri 
Rindeswar Mahato Mining Sirdar of Bhagaband colliery in 
connection with drainage of surface water which was posing 
danger of ennundation to the underground mino of Bhagaband 
colliery . It was stated that the concerned workman and Shr 
Govind Mahato attacked Shri K . P . Singh with are and lathi 
and they also hurled abuse upon him . Shri K , P . Singh 
hechme unconscione on Arcount of assault and he was 
drafted 10 village Dhobni Mouza with the intention that he 
he killed . It was stated furtbei Shri B . S . Ram and others 
flrd for their safety and gave information to the police , 
The Police cenie and rescued Shri K . P . Singh and arrested 
the concerned workman , 


9 . As stated above it is clear that the management was 
getting the work done at his own land . Drain culling job 
was be:ng done under the supervision of Shri K . L , Singo, 
Sr. Mining kagincer , Bhagaband Colliery. Shri Singa stated 
that the work was being done in the collery area , The work 
in itself was very important and closely connected with 
the mine for the water of the different collery including that 
or Moonidih colliery was channelised and diverted ic different 
direction to save ennundation of underground minc of Bhaga 
band Colliory . We find that the work was closely connecter 
with tho ali airs of mine and it was being done at about 
12 . 45 P . M . during the office hour . This cans that Shri K . P . 
Singh and another officials assisting him were on o ficial duty 
well within the premises of tho colliery . It is said that at 
that point of time the concerned workiran and oiberg came 
abusing and assaulted Shri K . P , Singh causing injuries to his 
various part of the body. Tho concerned workizan WWX 
charged for riotous bohaviour and also for the stoppage ont 
drainage work on the company s land with intention to drown 
Bhagaband Colliery . As alleged , the abovo act of tho con 
cerned workman amounted to misconduct under the certified 
standing orders which reads as follows : 


" 17(i)(e ) : Drunkenness , fighting or riotous, disorderly 

or indecent behaviour whilo on duty at the place 
of work . 


6 . The concerned morkman was issued chargeshent hy 
the Sundt. of Mines of Moonidih Project who was also the 
Manager under the Mince Act, 1952. The concerned Tork 
man suhmitted reply which was not found satisfactory and 
Homestic onquiry was held , Ultimately , he was dismissert , 
However . We find that the domestic enguiry was held to be 
unfair and not in rccordance with the princinlet of natural 
justice and the management was allowed to prove the charge 
hv adducing fresh evidence before this Court . 


17 (i)(i) : Causing wilful damage to work in progress par 

to property of the employer . 
170)(r) : Threatening , abousing or essaulting eny sapar 

ior or co -worker." 


10 . Tho concerned workman was definitely not on tuty 
but Shri K . P , Singh and other officials frere on duły working 
in the colliory promises or in the vicinity of tho premises .

THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : NOVEMBER 7, 1992 / KARTIKA 10 , 1914 


[PART 11 - - SEL . 3 ( ü ) ) 


. The learned counsel for the workmen at this stage submitted 
trat the management has got no extra territoriul jurisdiction 
and he cannot be a guru or mentor of his workmcn for their 
regulated cultural advancement. Reliance was placed upoti 
the authority reported in 1983 Supreine Court Lab I. C . Vol. 2 
page 1909 wherein their Lordships were pleased to hold as 
follows : 


. bani with the intcntion to be luled but Slui K . P , Singh 

himself stated that he was not assaulted after his arrest and 
taking away by the villagers . This is suggestive of the 
fact that the concerned workman and another hud 110vor 
intended to kill Shri Singlı . But more dragging will amount 
to assault. This story of dragging by the concerned work 
man has been stated by all the witnesses including Shri B , S , 
Ram . This witness also stated that the concerned workman 
hurled abuses upon Shri K . P . Singh and others . On the 
basis of the discussion made above I am to hold the view 
that the concerned workmun was quilty of misconduct under 
claugos 7 ( i) and ( r ) of the Cortified Standing Orders , 


" To enablo an amployer to peacefully carry on his mu 

dustrial activity , the Act confers powers on bim 
to proscribc conditions of service including enumo. 
rating acts of misconduct when committed within 
the premises of the establishment. The employer 
has hardly any extra territorial jurisdiction . He is 
not the custodian of general law and order situation 
nor the Guru or mentor of his workmen for their 
woll regulated cultural advancement . If the powor 
to regulate the behaviour of the workmen Outside 
tho duty hours and at any place wherever they may 
bo was conferred upon the employer , contract of 
service may be reduced to contract of slavery . The 
einployer is entitled to prescribe conditions of service 
more or less specifying the acts of misconduct to 
be enforced within the premises where the workmen 
gather togother for rendering service . The employer 
has both power and jurisdiction to regulate tho 
behaviour of workmon within the premises of the 
establishment, for peacefully carrying the industrial 
activity in the vicinity of the establishment." 


14 . It was lastly contended that a police case was also 
registerd for the same occurence but the charge cannot be 
proved and the accused concerned workman WAS acquitted , 
Shri K . P , Singh whilo doposing as witness stated that he 
was not examined as witness before the Criminal Court Defi 
nitely ho being the informant of the case was very important 
witness in the criminal trial but his non - examigation will 
simply suggest that the criminal trial was not fully contested 
by the State and for that the management cannot be held 
responsible. Certainly in order of the Criminal Court has 
to be respected and honoured just to maintain its dignity and 
also to maintain faith of the litigant public provided tho 
accused is either convicted or acquitted after a full dress 
trial. Here in the instant caso we find that the informant 
was not examined and therefore it cannot be said that the 
concerned workman got clean acquittal afrer full dress trial. 


11 . I have already stated that the work was going on at 
the place which was in the premises of the colliery and it 
was well within the vicinity of Bhagaband Colliery . The 
concerned workman was a minor / loader of Moonidih Col 
liory which was just in the vicinity of Bhagaband Colliery . 
The area of the coalfield for the purpose of any action 
against the delinquent employee cannot be confined to water 
tight compartment. According to Puttar Mahato tho con 
cerned workman Moonidih Colliery was at a distance of 
4 KM , from Bhagaband Collierv . Again he stated that 
Dhohani Mouza was at a distance of 2 KM . from Bhagaband 
Colliery . This will be suggestive of the fact that the distanco 
between the two collieries from each other was very short 
and everything happened within the premises of the colliory . 
Though the concerned workman was not on duty but ho as 
Altered obstructed the work of the officers on duty within 
colliery premises as such the act will embrace the ingredients 
of misconduct. The employer cannot ho guru or montor 
for any privato work of an employee but he will be definitely 
a mater for any obstruction or riotops behaviour being dono 
nt the colliery premises irrespoctive of the fact that the cmp 
loyee was on duty or not . 


15 . I have held the concerned workman guilty of mis 
conduct under clause 17 ( 1)( T ) of the Certified Standing Ordors 
which was an act of abusing and assuulting. I have explained 
that the management failed to prove murderous assault on 
the nerson of Shri K , P . Singh but the charge of simple 
assault has been proved simply because he was dragged by 
the villagers including the concerned workman to village 
Dhobani Mouza . But the misconduct as proved against tho 
concerned workman was not so serious as to inflict the punish 
ment of dismissal against the concerned workman . I may say 
it was shockingly disproportionate to the misconduct commi 
ted by the concerned workman . In the circumstances , I would 
set aside the oder of dismissal and the ends of justice can be 
met by roinstating the concerned workman without any hack 
wages and one rermanent stoppage of increment. As regarde 
increment is concerned his increment duc in future will be 
permanently withheld . The management is thus directed to 
reinstate the concerned workman within onc month from the 
date of publication of the Award with continuity of his servic . 
and stoppage of increment as indicated above. 


B . RAM , Presiding Officer

AWARD 
The Central Government vide its abovo cited order har 
referred the following dispute for adjudication to this 
Tribunal under Section 10 ( 1XD ) of the Industrial Disputes 
Act, 1947 : 


" Whether it is considered necessary to impose eficiency 

bur , the circumstances necessitating the proposed 
imposition of the bar should be communicated to 
the employee and the cmployee should be given an 
opportunity to submit tho explanation which should 
bo duly considered for a final decision regarding 

the imposition of efficiency bar." 
The Association submits that alongwith the instructions 
of the above circular the Corporation has issued the instruc 
tions for filling the confidential reports in form No. CR - 5 
on the work of the administrative stoff which leads as 
linder : 
" Wlicrcvcr any adverse report is place which is likely 

to affect the employee the reviewing office should 
communicate the same to the employee and give 
him an opportunity of explanation otally and / or 
in writing. The explanation given by the cm 
ployce should be filed along with the confidential 
report together with such remarks as the revicw 
ing officer considers necessary to make such cases 
should be brought to the notice of the appoint 

ing authority ." 
The plea of the Association in that inspite of the above cir 
culary and instructions thereunder and inspite of the 
judicial pronouncements, the Corporation with held the 
efficiency bar of Shri Meghwal falling on 1 - 2 - 85 without 
intimating him thc reasons for with holding the EB and 
without giving him chance to submit explanation for tho 
game which was against the Staff Regulaions and principle 
of natural justice , as as also in fagrant violation of the 
judicial pronouncements , therefore , being arbitrary and 
unjust and must be set aside, 


" Whether the action of the management of Life Insu 

rance Corporation of India , Ajmer Division Ajmer 
in not allowaing Shri P . C . Heghwal, Higher Grade 
Assistant to cross EB with effcct from 1- 2 - 1985 
is just and legal ? If not, to what relief the con 
cerned worker entitlod " . 


2 . The Northern Zone Insuranco Employees Association , 
Ajmer , hereafter referred to as the Association has espoused 
the disputo of Shri P . C . Meghwal, Higher Grade Assistant 
(HGA ) who bas been not allowed to cross the Efficience 
Bar (EB ) w . e. f. 1 - 2 - 85. The facts in short are that Shri 
Meghwal was HGA at Unit No. 1 in Udaipur Branch office 
of the Life Insurance Corporation of India . The pay scale 
of the HGA wag 775 -60 -1135 - EB - 75 - 235 , Shri Meghwal 
on getting his annual grade increment due on 1 - 2 - 84 reach 
ed the stage of 1135 in tho above pay scale and, therefore , 
he was to cross the EB before 1 - 2 - 85 in order to get next 
annual grade increment of Rs. 75 . The plea of the Associa

The sum total of the pronouncements referred to above 
is that in cases of promotion and supersoggion principles of 
Datural justice anply and the same cannot be based upon 
un - communicated confidential reports whereas the cases relat 
ino, to comnulsory retirement as well as withohlding of EB 
do not amount to punishment and, thereforo , they boing in 
tho nature of administrative orders and the subjective satis 
faction of tho competent authority is tho only consideration , 
theiefore, principles of natural justice do not apply and even 
if such orders aro hased on lin - communicated adverse entrios 
cannot be interfered with by the Courts .
